    part's name, the litres a second it lets in, what mends it; at a mast its sails and rigging, at the helm the rudder,
    at the pump the water. Does it fit, and read well?
  - **Mending:** hold right-click with planks and nails (or flax and resin, cloth, rope) on a hurt part: "Mending …%"
    above the hotbar for 4 s, the items taken (the hand first), the sound, the part sound again. Letting go or looking
    away stops it. Below the water: from the hold, and diving to her side.
  - **The pump** (in front of the forecastle): the hint, holding right-click (a stroke a second, the vanilla pump sound),
    the brake rocking (for others too), the water going down in the readout; two players at it.
  - **The helm's readout** with water in her: the water line, "She's foundering!", hurt sails and rudder; do four lines
    fit the box? Everyone aboard gets the foundering warning.
  - **Water in her** (`/magelan flood 200`): she sits deeper (and her waterline cut with her), lists, rolls slower; she's
    slower at the helm. The water inside is drawn (3b, below).
  - **Running aground** under sail at speed: the chat message (holed...), the sound, the damage at the bow; bumping on
    with sails set wears more.
  - **Sinking** (`/magelan flood 1200` in deep water): the warnings, being put off into the water, the rowing boats on
    the gallows floating free beside her, her going down (smooth? listing and by the head), lying on the bottom; diving
    to her and opening a chest in her hold; no dry cut-out left round her; no climbing back aboard. In shallow water:
    on the bottom with her deck above the water, `/magelan repairall`, and she floats off.
  - **Spawn:** bound to her at the plate, sink her: the "your ship has gone down" message and the old spawn back.
- From the overnight build (Magelan 0.3.5), 2. the ship's shadow (graphics settings: shadows on, a sunny day):
  - **Her shadow** on the water, the seabed in shallows, a dock or beach, the rowing boat alongside and the people
    aboard; it lines up with her (also while you're aboard and she rolls), and moves with the sails as they're set,
    furled and braced round.
  - **On herself:** the sails' and castles' shadows on the decks; no stripes, speckles or flicker on her sunlit hull
    and sails (shadow acne); no gap where the shadow of a mast or rail meets the deck.
  - The cabins and the lower deck by day: a little darker than before; still fine?
  - Near and far: at the near map's edge (about 30 blocks) the ropes' shadows go; far off the hull, sails and yards
    still shade the water.
  - With shadows at the lowest step (far map only), and off: no errors. The client log has no "[magelan] Ship shadows
    failed" line. `.magelan shadows off` and `on` to compare; the frame rate with her in view, on and off.
  - The launch (`skiff`, parked) and the rowing boat cast shadows too.
- From the overnight build (Magelan 0.3.5), 1. the crosshair and the spawn point:
  - **The crosshair on her:** the look-at box (top of the screen) says what you look at: from the water her hull
    ("Hull, port side, amidships, below the waterline (frames …)"), from aboard the decks, the undersides of the decks
    overhead, the masts, woodwork, the fixtures; it changes as you look along her, and with her rolling in a storm it
    still says what the crosshair is on. The client log should have no "Could not patch (the crosshair on ships)" or
    "(the look-at box on ships)" warnings.
  - No block highlight or placing through her hull or deck; blocks beside her (a dock, the sea over the rail) still
    selectable; eating and drinking while looking at the deck; no red "in reach" crosshair amidships; left-clicking
    her does nothing (no swing).
  - The rowing boat alongside: still selectable from the deck over the rail (Shift/Ctrl + right-click to pick her up),
    and the boat's seats and places from the water.
  - **The plate:** hold any metal plate on the quarterdeck: the place on the poop cabin's front, port of the door,
    shows a hint; right-click puts it up. How it looks (the vanilla plate stood on the wall, facing the wheel), its
    size and height.
  - Right-click it empty-handed: bound (chat says so, the hint and the look-at box too); again: unbound. Shift +
    right-click takes it down.
  - Bound, die at sea (or `/kill`): you come back standing on the quarterdeck by the plate, aboard (walking with her),
    also after she has sailed on far, crossed Cylinder World's seam, or you respawned with her chunk not loaded.
  - The old spawn back: after unbinding, after the plate is taken down, after `/magelan remove` (online, and for
    someone offline when they next join); a temporal gear used while bound ends the binding.
- From the fifth client test (Magelan 0.3.4 / Sea 0.3.2):
  - the rowing boat: sitting in her by clicking the thwart, and by stepping, jumping or swimming into her; which way
    you face; the oars; Ctrl + right-click with a chest or vessel puts it in her; picking her up from a deck or ladder
  - the carrack (the long one): the poop cabin 1 longer, the binnacle and its heading, no great cabin windows, the
    companionway and hatches on the centreline, the capstan aft of the mainmast with its cable along the deck, the
    pump; rolling under sail, and no water or bubbles on deck in a storm (`/sea setwind S 1.5`)
- From the fourth client test (Magelan 0.3.3 / Sea 0.3.2):
  - **The three carracks** (three charters): how the deep ones sit, the quarterdeck at the mainmast and the shrouds on
    its side, the overhanging poop cabin and its knees, the forecastle's raked underside and the figurehead under it,
    the gentler castle sweeps, room in the waist (13 / 15) and the wider hold; the water round their sterns (Sea's cut)
  - **The rowing boat:** which way you face on the thwart, the oars at rest and stroking, speed, no waves inside, stops
    against a ship's side, the places (chest, vessel, boat seat), picking up from the water, a deck and a rope ladder,
    two on a carrack's gallows (oars shipped)
  - the gates, stairs and forecastle windows of point 3–6 above; swimmers kept out of the hull at the waterline
- From the third client test (Magelan 0.3.2 / Sea 0.3.2; none seen in the client yet):
  - **The ship's boat:** putting her in from the item (bow the way you look), climbing in (swimming into her,

  - **The stern's water:** cut off square at the transom, nothing cut away beside her quarters (the raked cut).
  - **Wood grain** along the frames, beams, knees' neighbours, masts, yards and posts.
  - **Picking a boat off the gallows.**
  - **Aground:** digging free the rock or soil she ran into; backing off.
  - **Seaweed** gone from inside the hull as she sails over it.

- Vanilla blocks aboard: door hinge side and swing, chest facing, bed direction, glass-pane blending. None of these
  had been seen in the client when this list was written.
- Rope ladders: boarding from the water, climbing down into it, a shut gate at the top.
- From the 2026-09-29 changes (none seen in the client yet):
  - **Textures:** tar near black on the hull, black seams on the decks; the log line `[magelan] Hull textures in the
    atlas` (tarred and caulked oak should differ from plain oak).
  - **Moving parts:** the gates swinging inboard (and shut, flush with the bulwark), both hatches' leaves, the tiller
    and its ropes with the helm, the anchor going down and up with its cable, the yards bracing round with the sails
    staying under them, braces and sheets following, the lateen stopping at 30°.
  - **Vanilla meshes:** the raft on its chocks (size, which way round), the captain's bed (woodaged, head to the
    stern), the chart table and stools, the firepit on the hearth.
  - **Glass:** the stern gallery's lights (plain and stained) and the waist hatch's panes, blended.
  - **Walking and climbing:** the companionway ladder down and up, the hood (no head bumps), dropping through the open
    waist hatch, standing in the open hold hatch, the capstan ring, the castle-break bulwarks.
  - **The hold:** the scrolling dialog with 1560 slots, shift-clicking stacks in and out, the sync when several
    players look in.
  - **Boarding:** right-clicking the hull away from the ladders says where they are.
  - **Rooms:** rain on the lower deck only while the waist hatch is open.
- From item 18 (none seen in the client yet):
  - the frames, beams, knees and clamps everywhere inside (spacing, how they look against the planking, clipping into

  - the hold: down the ladder and back up (a shut hatch stops you at the top), walking round the cargo deck, the frames

  - no water, fog, tint or breath bar in the hold, nor on the lower deck in a storm's trough
  - the waterline from outside: the waves now run right up to the hull (no calm ring), and no water shows inside
    the hull or under the bottom's curve

- From the second client test (Magelan 0.3.1 / Sea 0.3.1; none seen in the client yet):
  - **Water:** the cut round the hull at the stern (tight to the transom), and in a gale with her heeling, no waves
    showing inside the lower deck; no bubbles or splashing walking in the hold. The client log's shader line should

  - **Nothing poking through the hull:** deck edges at the waterline and the bow, the castle sides, the gates, the

  - **Frames:** every 1.5, beefier; how they look against the planking and the beds and chests at the sides.

  - **Cargo:** a chest, a trunk across two places, a storage vessel, a crate in the hold (which way they face).

  - **Rigging:** furled sails with their sheets up at the yards; the lateen's straight edges; the bowsprit's heel.
  - **The wheel** turning smoothly.

## Later

- **Placing things aboard.** The user wants vanilla lanterns (and later mechanisms) placeable and working aboard.
  - Free placement of furniture on a grid, with keep-clear zones and fixed spots for fire, is the favourite.
  - Not decided yet.
- **Mechanisms aboard, rebuilt from vanilla meshes with our own logic**, not a hidden workshop room (the user ruled that
  out):
  - a treadwheel ("giant hamster wheel"), crank or pedals for power
  - a forge, a helve hammer, an ore smelter, so the crew have things to do on a voyage
  - The smithing minigame is the hard part to reuse. Needs research.
- **Repairs:** ship wear is now planned under "Damage, sinking and wrecks" (steps 4 and 6). Mending tools and gear by
  smithing aboard is still open.
- **Capstan by hand:** walk the bars to weigh the anchor; more players go faster.
- **Gangway:** a walkway from the entry port to a dock when the ship lies alongside.
- **Heating and cooking:** the firehearth in the forecastle works as vanilla's firepit (the sixth client test, 8: built
  2026-10-02, not seen in the client). Still open: fire spreading aboard (a hearth knocked over in a storm, a cannon ball
  through it), a galley stove or a bread oven, feeding it from the hold, the cooking smells and the crew's meals.
- **The anchor hangs in the ship's frame:** when she rolls, the hanging anchor tilts with her instead of hanging plumb.
- **Bigger picture:** slipway construction, more ships (galleon, man-o'-war with a double wheel), multiplayer testing.

- Afterwards (main session): taking a panel out afloat now needs half a block of freeboard (sill ≥ waterline + sinkage + 0.5, was + 0.1) — waves and heel reach band 4 panels just over the waterline. Self-test 361 ALL OK, offline 3 known failures.
